def solve():
    import sys
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    it = iter(data)
    n = next(it); m = next(it)
    adj = [[] for _ in range(n+1)]
    for _ in range(m):
        u = next(it); v = next(it)
        adj[u].append(v)
        adj[v].append(u)
    color = [0]*(n+1)
    for i in range(1,n+1):
        if color[i]==0:
            color[i]=1
            stack=[i]
            while stack:
                u=stack.pop()
                cu=color[u]
                nv = 2 if cu==1 else 1
                for v in adj[u]:
                    if color[v]==0:
                        color[v]=nv
                        stack.append(v)
                    elif color[v]==cu:
                        print("NO")
                        return
    sys.stdout.write("YES\n")
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(str(color[i]) for i in range(1,n+1)) + "\n")

Let's produce final code:

import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    n, m = data[0], data[1]
    adj = [[] for _ in range(n + 1)]
    idx = 2
    for _ in range(m):
        u = data[idx]; v = data[idx+1]; idx += 2
        adj[u].append(v)
        adj[v].append(u)

    color = [0] * (n + 1)
    for start in range(1, n + 1):
        if color[start] == 0:
            color[start] = 1
            stack = [start]
            while stack:
                u = stack.pop()
                cu = color[u]
                opp = 2 if cu == 1 else 1
                for v in adj[u]:
                    if color[v] == 0:
                        color[v] = opp
                        stack.append(v)
                    elif color[v] == cu:
                        sys.stdout.write("NO\n")
                        return
    sys.stdout.write("YES\n")
    sys.stdout.write(" ".join(map(str, color[1:])) + "\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()
